lesson: Adjectives - Quality, Part 16
lang: ja
english: sour
current: サワー
proposed: すっぱい
kind: untranslated
reason: The word 'サワー' is a loanword usually referring to a type of drink (like a sour beer), whereas the standard Japanese word for the taste of sour is 'すっぱい'.
source: Wiktionary (Japanese), s.v. "すっぱい"
checked by: mistral:7b > gemma4:12b, confirmed by Claude
status: shipped 2026-11-11
